THE PATH IS THE ELEMENT ITSELF, not one surface's parent chain (matched as a substring, so a descendant of such an element matches too — the value-and-properties coupling below is what still bounds the family): the first spelling required a `gap-3` parent (the projects form column's own class), and the find sheet renders the same primitive directly in the sheet's content column, whose path carries no `gap-3` ancestor — so the step failed there (every find cell, both gap properties, on this slice's own capture) while the app-wide sweep it waits for is unchanged.
